## Recorded semantic-review policy

Clause-by-clause source-type review against pinned manuscript, independent of compilation, exact-type manifest, and value-use receipts.

- <code>corrected</code>: Explicit correction to manuscript mathematical notation or hypotheses.
- <code>exact</code>: Mapped statement covers the specified complete identity, including definitionally equivalent coordinates.
- <code>generalized</code>: Same specified identity with weaker hypotheses, broader domain, or explicit equivalent parametrization.
- <code>normalized&#95;reformulation</code>: Actual equivalent finite-kernel/path/expectation formula; normalization and bridge obligations are explicitly identified.
- <code>partial&#95;display</code>: Only identified clause(s) or the final conclusion are in this mapped type; intermediate displayed equalities must not be credited as a whole.
- <code>proof&#95;improvement</code>: Substantive replacement argument, not a claim to contain the literal printed calculation.
- <code>specialized</code>: Specified display or clause under a restricted chart, domain, or coordinate representation that is explicit in formula&#95;scope.
- <code>step&#95;component</code>: Only identified substantive component of a named proof step, not its entire construction.
